2025-06-18T11:55:44.7234016Z <pr_title>
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7234820Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7235986Z </pr_title>
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7236297Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7236528Z <pr_description>
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7237877Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7239198Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7239638Z ## Problem
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7240741Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7241636Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7242269Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7242898Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7243599Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7244243Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7244797Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7245356Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7246557Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7247630Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7247790Z ## Solution
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7248162Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7248512Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7249131Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7249627Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7250490Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7252366Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7253722Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7254338Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7254806Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7255527Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7255886Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7256235Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7256770Z ```bash
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7257757Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7258872Z ```
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7259060Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7259231Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7259744Z ```bash
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7260992Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7262473Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7262993Z ```
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7263221Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7263594Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7264053Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7264243Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7265615Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7266767Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7267071Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7267553Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7268509Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7269494Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7270492Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7271577Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7272164Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7272791Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7273591Z 
2025-06-18T11:55:44.7273725Z Fixes #11.
